:app-name: PokéAPI

[abstract]
.Abstract
All the Pokémon data you'll ever need in one place, easily accessible through a modern free open-source RESTful API.  ## What is this?  This is a full RESTful API linked to an extensive database detailing everything about the Pokémon main game series.  We've covered everything from Pokémon to Berry Flavors.  ## Where do I start?  We have awesome [documentation](https://pokeapi.co/docs/v2) on how to use this API. It takes minutes to get started.  This API will always be publicly available and will never require any extensive setup process to consume.  Created by [**Paul Hallett**](https://github.com/phalt) and other [**PokéAPI contributors**](https://github.com/PokeAPI/pokeapi#contributing) around the world. Pokémon and Pokémon character names are trademarks of Nintendo.


// markup not found, no include::{specDir}intro.adoc[opts=optional]

[.Berries]
=== Berries


[.berryFirmnessList]
==== berryFirmnessList

`GET /api/v2/berry-firmness/`

List berry firmness

===== Description

Berries can be soft or hard. Check out [Bulbapedia](http://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Category:Berries_by_firmness) for greater detail.


// markup not found, no include::{specDir}api/v2/berry-firmness/GET/spec.adoc[opts=optional]



===== Parameters





====== Query Parameters

[cols="2,3,1,1,1"]
|===
|Name| Description| Required| Default| Pattern

| limit
| Number of results to return per page. 
| -
| null
| 

| offset
| The initial index from which to return the results. 
| -
| null
| 

| q
| &gt; Only available locally and not at [pokeapi.co](https://pokeapi.co/docs/v2) Case-insensitive query applied on the &#x60;name&#x60; property.  
| -
| null
| 

|===


===== Return Type

<<PaginatedBerryFirmnessSummaryList>>


===== Content Type

* application/json

===== Responses

[cols="2,3,1"]
|===
| Code | Message | Datatype


| 200
| 
|  <<PaginatedBerryFirmnessSummaryList>>

|===

[.berryFlavorList]
==== berryFlavorList

`GET /api/v2/berry-flavor/`

===== Description

[.berryList]
==== berryList

`GET /api/v2/berry/`

List berries

===== Description

Berries are small fruits that can provide HP and status condition restoration, stat enhancement, and even damage negation when eaten by Pokémon. Check out [Bulbapedia](http://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Berry) for greater detail.

[.characteristicList]
==== characteristicList

`GET /api/v2/characteristic/`

List characteristics

===== Description

Characteristics indicate which stat contains a Pokémon's highest IV. A Pokémon's Characteristic is determined by the remainder of its highest IV divided by 5 (gene_modulo). Check out [Bulbapedia](http://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Characteristic) for greater detail.

[.pokemonList]
==== pokemonList

`GET /api/v2/pokemon/`

List pokemon

===== Description

Pokémon are the creatures that inhabit the world of the Pokémon games. They can be caught using Pokéballs and trained by battling with other Pokémon. Each Pokémon belongs to a specific species but may take on a variant which makes it differ from other Pokémon of the same species, such as base stats, available abilities and typings. See [Bulbapedia](http://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_(species)) for greater detail.

[.pokemonSpeciesList]
==== pokemonSpeciesList

`GET /api/v2/pokemon-species/`

List pokemon species

===== Description

A Pokémon Species forms the basis for at least one Pokémon. Attributes of a Pokémon species are shared across all varieties of Pokémon within the species. A good example is Wormadam; Wormadam is the species which can be found in three different varieties, Wormadam-Trash, Wormadam-Sandy and Wormadam-Plant.

| id

| 
| X
|   Integer

| X
| X
|   Integer

|     

| pp
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| Power points. The number of times this move can be used.
|     

| priority
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| A value between -8 and 8. Sets the order in which moves are executed during battle. See [Bulbapedia](http://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Priority) for greater detail.
|     

| power
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The base power of this move, or null if it has no fixed base power.

| X
| X
| <<MoveCombos>>

| X
| X
| <<ContestTypeSummary>>

| X

| <<ContestEffectSummary>>    
| The effect the move has when used in a contest.
|     

| damage_class
| X
|

| The type of damage the move inflicts on the target, e.g. physical.
|

| X
| 
|   List   of <<MoveEffectEffectText>>
| The effect of this move listed in different languages.
|     

| effect_changes
| X
| 
|   List   of <<MoveEffectChange>>

|     

| generation
| X
| 
| <<GenerationSummary>>    
| The generation in which this move was introduced.
|     

| meta
| X
| X
| <<MoveMeta>>    
| Metadata about this move
|     

| names
| X
| 
|   List   of <<MoveName>>
| The name of this resource listed in different languages.
|     

| past_values
| X
| 
|   List   of <<MoveChange>>
| A list of move resource value changes across version groups of the game.
|     

| stat_changes

|

| <<SuperContestEffectSummary>>

|     

| target
| X
|

| X
| 
| <<TypeSummary>>    
| The elemental type of this move.
|

|

|

|

[#PokemonEvolution]
=== _PokemonEvolution_ 




[.fields-PokemonEvolution]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| version_group
| X
| 
| <<VersionGroupSummary>>    
| The version group in which these evolution requirements apply.
|     

| is_default
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether the evolution is considered as the expected evolution in a main series game. Each unique Pokémon variety of a line capable of evolution should have exactly one &#39;default&#39; evolution. For example, the Meowth species has three default evolutions as there are three distinct varieties it can evolve into: Persian, Alolan Persian, and Perrserker.
|     

| item
| X
| X
| <<ItemSummary>>    
| The item required to cause evolution this into Pokémon species.
|     

| trigger
| X
| 
| <<EvolutionTriggerSummary>>    
| The type of event that triggers evolution into this Pokémon species.
|     

| gender
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The id of the gender of the evolving Pokémon species must be in order to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| held_item
| X
| X
| <<ItemSummary>>    
| The item the evolving Pokémon species must be holding during the evolution trigger event to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| known_move
| X
| 
| <<PokemonEvolution_known_move>>    
| 
|     

| known_move_type
| X
| 
| <<PokemonEvolution_known_move>>    
| 
|     

| location
| X
| X
| <<LocationSummary>>    
| The location the evolution must be triggered at.
|     

| min_level
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum required level of the evolving Pokémon species to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| min_happiness
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum required level of happiness the evolving Pokémon species to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| min_beauty
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum required level of beauty the evolving Pokémon species to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| min_affection
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum required level of affection the evolving Pokémon species to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| near_special_rock
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not you need to be near a Moss Rock or Icy Rock to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| needs_multiplayer
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not multiplayer link play is needed to evolve into this Pokémon species (e.g. Union Circle).
|     

| needs_overworld_rain
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not it must be raining in the overworld to cause evolution this Pokémon species.
|     

| party_species
| X
| X
| <<PokemonSpeciesSummary>>    
| The Pokémon species that must be in the players party in order for the evolving Pokémon species to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| party_type
| X
| X
| <<TypeSummary>>    
| The player must have a Pokémon of this type in their party during the evolution trigger event in order for the evolving Pokémon species to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| relative_physical_stats
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The required relation between the Pokémon&#39;s Attack and Defense stats. 1 means Attack &gt; Defense. 0 means Attack &#x3D; Defense. -1 means Attack &lt; Defense.
|     

| time_of_day
| 
| 
|   String  
| The required time of day. Day or night.
|     

| trade_species
| X
| X
| <<PokemonSpeciesSummary>>    
| Pokémon species for which this one must be traded.
|     

| turn_upside_down
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not the 3DS needs to be turned upside-down as this Pokémon levels up.
|     

| region
| X
| X
| <<RegionSummary>>    
| The required region in which this evolution can occur.
|     

| required_pokemon_form
| X
| X
| <<PokemonFormSummary>>    
| The Pokémon form required before evolving, or null if unrestricted.
|     

| evolved_pokemon_form
| X
| X
| <<PokemonFormSummary>>    
| The Pokémon form produced by this evolution, or null if unspecified.
|     

| used_move
| X
| X
| <<MoveSummary>>    
| The move that must be used by the evolving Pokémon species during the evolution trigger event in order to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| min_move_count
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum number of times a move must be used in order to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| min_steps
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum number of steps that must be taken in order to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| min_damage_taken
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The minimum amount of damage taken during the evolution trigger event in order to evolve into this Pokémon species.
|     

| allowed_natures
| X
| X
|   List   of <<NatureSummary>>
| The natures that allow this evolution, or null if unrestricted.
|     

| condition_expression
| X
| X
| <<EvolutionConditionExpression>>    
| An additional evolution condition expressed in reverse Polish notation, or null if none.
|     

|===

|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| id

| 
| X
|   Integer

| 
| 
|   Boolean

| form_names

| names

|     

| types

|

|     

| trigger_conditions

| flavor_text_entries

[#PokemonSpeciesDetail]
=== _PokemonSpeciesDetail_ 




[.fields-PokemonSpeciesDetail]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| id
| X
| 
|   Integer  
| The identifier for this resource.
|     

| name
| X
| 
|   String  
| The name for this resource.
|     

| order
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The order in which species should be sorted. Based on National Dex order, except families are grouped together and sorted by stage.
|     

| gender_rate
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The chance of this Pokémon being female, in eighths; or -1 for genderless.
|     

| capture_rate
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The base capture rate; up to 255. The higher the number, the easier the catch.
|     

| base_happiness
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| The happiness when caught by a normal Pokéball; up to 255. The higher the number, the happier the Pokémon.
|     

| is_baby
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not this is a baby Pokémon.
|     

| is_legendary
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not this is a legendary Pokémon.
|     

| is_mythical
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not this is a mythical Pokémon.
|     

| hatch_counter
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| Initial hatch counter: one must walk Y × (hatch_counter + 1) steps before this Pokémon&#39;s egg hatches, unless utilizing bonuses like Flame Body&#39;s. Y varies per generation. In Generations II, III, and VII, Egg cycles are 256 steps long. In Generation IV, Egg cycles are 255 steps long. In Pokémon Brilliant Diamond and Shining Pearl, Egg cycles are also 255 steps long, but are shorter on special dates. In Generations V and VI, Egg cycles are 257 steps long. In Pokémon Sword and Shield, and in Pokémon Scarlet and Violet, Egg cycles are 128 steps long.
|     

| has_gender_differences
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not this Pokémon has visual gender differences.
|     

| forms_switchable
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether or not this Pokémon has multiple forms and can switch between them.
|     

| growth_rate
| X
| 
| <<GrowthRateSummary>>    
| The rate at which this Pokémon species gains levels.
|     

| pokedex_numbers
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonDexEntry>>
| A list of Pokedexes and the indexes reserved within them for this Pokémon species.
|     

| egg_groups
| X
| 
|   List   of <<EggGroupSummary>>
| A list of egg groups this Pokémon species is a member of.
|     

| color
| X
| 
| <<PokemonColorSummary>>    
| The color of this Pokémon for Pokédex search.
|     

| shape
| X
| 
| <<PokemonShapeSummary>>    
| The shape of this Pokémon for Pokédex search.
|     

| evolves_from_species
| X
| X
| <<PokemonSpeciesSummary>>    
| The Pokémon species that evolves into this Pokemon_species.
|     

| evolution_chain
| X
| 
| <<EvolutionChainSummary>>    
| The evolution chain this Pokémon species is a member of.
|     

| habitat
| X
| X
| <<PokemonHabitatSummary>>    
| The habitat this Pokémon species can be encountered in.
|     

| generation
| X
| 
| <<GenerationSummary>>    
| The generation this Pokémon species was introduced in.
|     

| names
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonSpeciesName>>
| The name of this resource listed in different languages.
|     

| pal_park_encounters
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonSpeciesPalParkEncounter>>
| A list of encounters that can be had with this Pokémon species in pal park.
|     

| form_descriptions
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonSpeciesDescription>>
| Descriptions of different forms Pokémon take on within the Pokémon species.
|     

| flavor_text_entries
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonSpeciesFlavorText>>
| A list of flavor text entries for this Pokémon species.
|     

| genera
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonSpeciesGenus>>
| The genus of this Pokémon species listed in multiple languages.
|     

| varieties
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokemonSpeciesVariety>>
| A list of the Pokémon that exist within this Pokémon species.
|     

|===

| The game version this flavor text is extracted from.
|

[#StatDetail]
=== _StatDetail_ 




[.fields-StatDetail]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| id
| X
| 
|   Integer  
| The identifier for this resource.
|     

| name
| X
| 
|   String  
| The name for this resource.
|     

| game_index
| X
| 
|   Integer  
| ID the games use for this stat.
|     

| is_battle_only
| 
| 
|   Boolean  
| Whether this stat only exists within a battle.
|     

| affecting_moves
| X
| 
| <<StatAffectingMoves>>    
| A detail of moves which affect this stat positively or negatively.
|     

| affecting_natures
| X
| 
| <<StatAffectingNatures>>    
| A detail of natures which affect this stat positively or negatively.
|     

| affecting_items
| X
| 
|   List   of <<ItemSummary>>
| Items that affect this stat, such as vitamins and battle items.
|     

| characteristics
| X
| 
|   List   of <<CharacteristicSummary>>
| A list of characteristics that are set on a Pokémon when its highest base stat is this stat.
|     

| move_damage_class
| X
| X
| <<MoveDamageClassSummary>>    
| The class of damage this stat is directly related to.
|     

| names
| X
| 
|   List   of <<StatName>>
| The name of this resource listed in different languages.
|     

|===

[#TypeDetail]
=== _TypeDetail_ 

Serializer for the Type resource


[.fields-TypeDetail]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| id
| X
| 
|   Integer  
| The identifier for this resource.
|     

| name
| X
| 
|   String  
| The name for this resource.
|     

| damage_relations
| X
| 
| <<TypeRelationships>>    
| A detail of how effective this type is toward others and vice versa.
|     

| past_damage_relations
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypePastRelationships>>
| A list of details of how effective this type was toward others and vice versa in previous generations
|     

| game_indices
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeGameIndex>>
| A list of game indices relevent to this item by generation.
|     

| generation
| X
| 
| <<GenerationSummary>>    
| The generation this type was introduced in.
|     

| move_damage_class
| X
| X
| <<MoveDamageClassSummary>>    
| The class of damage inflicted by this type.
|     

| names
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeName>>
| The name of this resource listed in different languages.
|     

| pokemon
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypePokemon>>
| A list of details of Pokémon that have this type.
|     

| moves
| X
| 
|   List   of <<MoveSummary>>
| A list of moves that have this type.
|     

| sprites
| X
| 
|   Map   of <<map>>
| Type icons grouped by generation and game, with name_icon and symbol_icon URLs. Unavailable icons are null.
|     

|===

[.fields-TypeGameIndex]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| game_index
| X
|

| The internal id of an API resource within game data.
|     

| generation

| 
| <<GenerationSummary>>    
| The generation relevent to this game index.
|     

|===

[.fields-TypeGameSprite]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| name_icon
| 
| X
|   String  
| The URL of the icon displaying the type name in this game, or null if unavailable.

| 
| X

[#TypePastRelationships]
=== _TypePastRelationships_ 




[.fields-TypePastRelationships]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| generation
| X
| 
| <<GenerationSummary>>    
| The last generation in which the referenced type had the listed damage relations
|

| 
| <<TypeRelationships>>    
| The damage relations the referenced type had up to and including the listed generation

[.fields-TypePokemon]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===

| slot
| X
| 
|   Integer

[#TypeRelationships]
=== _TypeRelationships_ 




[.fields-TypeRelationships]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| no_damage_to
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeSummary>>
| A list of types this type has no effect on.
|     

| half_damage_to
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeSummary>>
| A list of types this type is not very effect against.
|     

| double_damage_to
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeSummary>>
| A list of types this type is very effect against.
|     

| no_damage_from
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeSummary>>
| A list of types that have no effect on this type.
|     

| half_damage_from
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeSummary>>
| A list of types that are not very effective against this type.
|     

| double_damage_from
| X
| 
|   List   of <<TypeSummary>>
| A list of types that are very effective against this type.
|     

|===

[#VersionDetail]
=== _VersionDetail_ 

Should have a link to Version Group info but the Circular
dependency and compilation order fight eachother and I&#39;m
not sure how to add anything other than a hyperlink


[.fields-VersionDetail]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| id
| X
| 
|   Integer  
| The identifier for this resource.
|     

| name
| X
| 
|   String  
| The name for this resource.
|     

| names
| X
| 
|   List   of <<VersionName>>
| The name of this resource listed in different languages.
|     

| version_group
| X
| 
| <<VersionGroupSummary>>    
| The version group this version belongs to.
|     

|===



[#VersionGroupDetail]
=== _VersionGroupDetail_ 




[.fields-VersionGroupDetail]
[cols="2,1,1,2,4,1"]
|===
| Field Name| Required| Nullable | Type| Description | Format

| id
| X
| 
|   Integer  
| The identifier for this resource.
|     

| name
| X
| 
|   String  
| The name for this resource.
|     

| order
| 
| X
|   Integer  
| Order for sorting. Almost by date of release, except similar versions are grouped together.
|     

| generation
| X
| 
| <<GenerationSummary>>    
| The generation this version was introduced in.
|     

| move_learn_methods
| X
| 
|   List   of <<MoveLearnMethodSummary>>
| A list of methods in which Pokémon can learn moves in this version group.
|     

| pokedexes
| X
| 
|   List   of <<PokedexSummary>>
| A list of Pokédexes introduces in this version group.
|     

| regions
| X
| 
|   List   of <<RegionSummary>>
| A list of regions that can be visited in this version group.
|     

| versions
| X
| 
|   List   of <<VersionSummary>>
| The versions this version group owns.
|     

|===
